1. `smooth_lse_obj_grad_z(z, beta)` for log domain. Use M = next_power_of_two(2n-1). Compute a = stable_softmax(z). Compute b = ifft(rfft(a,M)^2). Objective = scale*bmax + scale*logsumexp/beta.
But if a from softmax, z arbitrary; stable softmax:
z_shift = z - max(z); e=exp(z_shift); a=e/sum(e). To avoid overflow for very large negative, okay. To keep sum=1.
We may need to center z: z = z - mean(z) to reduce magnitude; softmax invariant.
Potential issue: objective flat along z shift; L-BFGS may struggle. We center inside objective? If objective not exactly invariant due floating, but we can return gradient with s projection. 2. `nesterov_log_lse(z, beta_start, beta_end, max_time)`: Custom accelerated gradient in log domain. Might be robust and fast. We can implement:
z = log(a)-mean; v=zeros; lr adaptive; mu.
For each iter:
  y = z + mu*v; y = y - mean(y)
  a = softmax(y)
  score, grad_z = lse_grad_z(y,beta)
  gnorm = norm(grad_z)
  step = lr/gnorm? Barzilli? Update v = mu v - lr*grad_z; z = z + v; center.
  Track exact score every e.g. 50.
Need choose lr. Scale grad_z ~? If objective F=2nLSE ~1.5, grad_z maybe O(0.01?) Let's estimate: With n=7000, a~1/n=1.4e-4, grad_score_a maybe 2n * gradient of b ~ O(1)? For active peak b~1/n, derivative wrt a pair ~a~1/n, sum few => O(1/n), times 2n~O(1). grad_z = a*grad ~1e-4. So lr maybe 1000 to move z by O(1). But z represents log ratios; O(1) changes weights factor e. Adaptive: set lr = target_delta / (0.5*gnorm?) If gnorm ~0.1, step ~? We can normalize gradient: direction = -grad_z/gnorm. Putative step size step_param = maybe 0.3 (log ratio change) using line search? Instead of fixed lr, use direction and backtracking on a(λ)=exp? z_new = y - λ*grad_z/gnorm; project? We can do line search over λ in log-domain (projected to z) with exact/LSE score. Since z-space unconstrained, choose λ scale. Starting λ=0.5, decrease if score increases. This can be robust. Use Nesterov with v in unit-norm direction? Simpler: projected subgradient with line search:
 z current; compute grad_gn. direction = -grad_z/norm.
 For λ = lambda_scale: candidate z = y + λ*direction? Actually update z_new = y + step*direction; normalize softmax; score. If better, accept and set step maybe; else reduce.
This is heavy if line search many evaluations. But maybe okay.

Implement `softmax_from_z(z)`:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size==0: return np.array([])
    zmax=np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
    return e/s
```

    z=np.log(a)
    z -= np.mean(z)
    return z
```

`grad_lse_from_p(a,p,M3)`? Function `lse_grad_L(a,p,M3)`:
```
def lse_grad_L(a,p,M3):
    n=len(a); C=2*n-1
    p_rev=np.zeros(M3); p_rev[:C]=p[::-1]
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M3); Pr=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,M3)
    r=np.fft.irfft(A*Pr,M3)[:3*n-2]
    return 2.0 * r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Then in objective, we already have A for a; can reuse? We compute A for B. We can also use A to multiply Pr. Good.
```
A = rfft(a,M3)
b = irfft(A*A,M3)[:C]
...
Pr = rfft(p_rev,M3)
r = irfft(A*Pr,M3) # A already fft; no need recompute.
```
This saves one rfft. Nice. Need if b clipped after computing A unaffected.

Implement:
```
def extract_best_window(x, n):
    M=len(x)
    if M<n: x=np.pad? no M>n.
    x_pos=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    # cyclic window sums: create xx=x_pos + x_pos[:M-1]? For shifts 0..M-1, window of length n sum = prefix.
    xx = np.concatenate([x_pos, x_pos[:M-1]])
    pref = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(xx)])
    sums = pref[n:n+M] - pref[:M] # if xx length 2M-1, n+M <=? xx length 2M-1, n≤M/2? n ~ M/3, okay
    s = int(np.argmax(sums))
    return np.maximum(x[s:s+n] if s+n<=M else np.concatenate([x[s:],x[:s+n-M]]),0.0)
```
M3=next pow2 3n, n <= M/3, so s+n <= 2n <=2M/3 <2M-1 ok.
Then normalize.

def build_fourier_candidate(n, rng, max_harm=8):
  x=np.arange(n)
  z=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,max_harm+1):
    amp = rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)/(m**0.7)
    phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    alpha = rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)
    z += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x/n + phase) + alpha*x
  a=np.exp(z); a=np.maximum(a,0); normalize
```

Let's implement target shape generator:
```
def trapezoid_target(C, plateau_frac):
   x=np.arange(C)
   # if C=1
   ramp=(1-plateau_frac)/2
   y=np.ones(C)
   # edge taper: for first ramp*C points linearly from 0 to1? Since edges at both ends zero? discrete b0 can't be zero if a0>0, but target can.
   if ramp>0:
      m=max(1,int(ramp*C))
      edge=np.linspace(0,1,m)
      y[:m]=edge
      y[-m:]=edge[::-1]
   y = np.maximum(y,0)
   if y.sum()<=0: y=np.ones(C)
   y/=y.sum()
   return y
```

Let's design final optimizer sequence in detail:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0=monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-30
    a = parent or default random length 6962
    best_a = normalize_nonneg(a); best_sc=exact
    # quick start basins
    starts=[best_a]
    for sig in [0.01,0.03,0.08,0.2]: starts.append(noisy(best_a,sig))
    for shape in [trapezoid_factor...]: starts.append(shape)
    # quick local on each with short time
    for a0 in starts:
       sc,a1=run_lbfgs_log(a0,'lse',50,maxiter=80,...) if time
       if improved update
       sc,a2=run_lbfgs_log(a0,'topk',32,maxiter=80)
       update
    # deep on best
    # topk high precision:
    for K in [128,64,32,16,8]:
       if time > 20:
          sc,a=run_lbfgs_log(best_a,'topk',K,maxiter=300,...)
          update
    # LSE continuation:
    for beta in [100,300,1000,3000,10000,30000,100000]:
       if time > 30:
          sc,a=run_lbfgs_log(best_a,'lse',beta,maxiter=500,...)
          update
       # intersperse topk 1
       if time > 20:
          sc,a=run_lbfgs_log(best_a,'topk',1,maxiter=200)
          update
    # spectral factor escape in middle
    if remaining > 200:
       for p in [0.8,0.7,0.6]:
          a_f=spectral_factor_from_b(best_a, p, steps=30)
          update; then quick lbfgs on a_f
    # maybe run a different start if time left
    # final polish with beta huge
```
Need manage maxiter to fit time. Since each L-BFGS call doesn't check deadline internally, choose conservative maxiter. We can estimate eval times by measuring a few exact scores? At start, calibrate:

t_start; m=5; for i in range(m): exact_score(best_a); avg=(monotonic()-t_start)/m
```

Simpler fixed moderate: 300 iterations per run. If too slow, we can't know. n=6962, objective cost with M3=32768 maybe ~0.005s? 300*1.5? ~1.5s. Safe. 1e-4? Let's be conservative: 100-300.

def minimize_callback(res):

Now, let's design `run_lbfgs_log` carefully:
```
def run_lbfgs_log(a, method, param, maxiter=500, maxfun=5000):
    a0=normalize_nonneg(a)
    z0=z_from_a(a0)
    if method=='lse': fun=lse_scipy; args=(float(param),)
    else fun=topk_scipy; args=(int(param),)
    try:
       res = minimize(fun, z0, jac=True, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B',
                      options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10,'maxls':30})
       a_new=softmax_from_z(res.x)
    except Exception as e:
       return exact_score(a0), a0
    sc=exact_score(a_new)
    if not finite: return exact_score(a0), a0
    return sc,a_new
```
If final worse than a0, caller will keep best. But we might want to use final anyway for next run even if worse? Could improve later; but if significantly worse, maybe not. We can return `a_new` and caller decides to update best; for continuation, maybe use a_new if not too worse? The `run_lbfgs_log` can return `a_new` regardless; caller has best. Smooth random Fourier:
```
def build_fourier_random(n, rng, max_harm=12):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,max_harm+1):
      if rng.random()<0.1: continue
      amp=rng.uniform(0.1,1.2)/(m**0.8)
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      trend=rng.uniform(-0.4,0.4)
      z += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + phase) + trend*x/m
   z = z - z.mean()
   z = np.clip(z, -5,5)
   a=np.exp(z); normalize
```
This smooth might be non-symmetric due phases/trend.

Let's implement `choose_sqrt_B(B, Aref)`:
```
def choose_sqrt_B(B, Aref):
  R = np.sqrt(B.astype(np.complex128))
  # DC positive
  R[0] = np.abs(R[0]) if R[0].imag small; if R[0].real <0: R[0]=-R[0]
  # For k=1 to len-2
  for k in range(1, len(B)-1):
     if abs(abs(R[k]-Aref[k]) - abs(-R[k]-Aref[k]))? choose sign
     if np.abs(-R[k] - Aref[k]) < np.abs(R[k] - Aref[k]): R[k] = -R[k]
  # Nyquist k=len-1
  k=len(B)-1
  # enforce real: r=np.sqrt(np.real(B[k])) with sign
  r=np.sqrt(max(0.0, np.real(B[k])))
  if np.imag(B[k]) large? But rfft Nyquist real; small imag due target not symmetric? target real padded -> rfft Nyquist real. 
  if np.abs(-r - Aref[k].real) < np.abs(r - Aref[k].real): r=-r
  R[k]=r+0j
  return R
```
Loop over k up to M/2 ~16384, each Python loop might be okay per factor step (15*16k=240k) fine. Could vectorize sign: sign = np.where((R-Aref).abs() < (R+Aref).abs(), 1, -1)? Actually choose R if |R-A| >=? We want min |±R-A|. R good if |R-A| <= |-R-A|. Vectorized:
```
d_use = np.abs(R - Aref)
d_flip = np.abs(-R - Aref)
R = np.where(d_flip < d_use, -R, R)
```
Then fix DC/Nyq. Good.

Let's write code for objective functions:
```
def objective_lse(z, beta):
    z = sanitize_z(z)
    a = softmax_from_z(z)
    n = len(a)
    C = 2*n -1
    M = get_fft_len_3n(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    B = A*A
    b = np.fft.irfft(B, M)[:C]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bsum = b.sum()
    # Could normalize b to sum1? a sum1, numerical bsum close 1. For score, self conv sum =1 exactly mathematically, but numerical. If not, using b as is yields LSE approximate max b; exact score uses max b/S^2. Since a normalized, S=1. If bsum slightly not 1, maybe scale b to sum1 for smooth? But b is conv of normalized a; should sum (sum a)^2=1. FFT numerical small. Scaling would alter objective slightly. We can leave.
    bmax = float(b.max())
    if not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    shifted = beta*(b-bmax)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700, 0)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1; L=bmax
    else: p=e/s; L=bmax+np.log(s)/beta
    grad_L = grad_L_from_A(A, p, M, n) # A reuse
    scale=2.0*n
    g_a = scale*grad_L
    dot = float(np.dot(a,g_a))
    g_z = a*(g_a - dot)
    if not all finite: ...
    g_z = g_z - np.mean(g_z) # remove shift
    val = scale*L
    if not finite val: return 1e100, zeros
    return val,g_z
```
`grad_L_from_A(A,p,M,n)`:
```
def grad_L_from_A(A, p, M, n):
    C=2*n-1
    p_rev=np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    p_rev[:C]=p[::-1]
    Pr=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,M)
    r=np.fft.irfft(A*Pr, M)
    return 2.0*r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```

def softmax_from_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, float)
    if p.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    if not finite: return normalize ones
    n=p.size+1
    maxp=np.maximum(0.0, np.max(p)) # since z0=0
    gp=p.copy(); # length n-1
    # e0 = exp(-maxp)
    e_rest = np.exp(p - maxp)
    e0 = np.exp(-maxp)
    s = e0 + e_rest.sum()
    if s <=0: return ones
    a = np.empty(n); a[0]=e0/s; a[1:]=e_rest/s
    return a

def run_lbfgs_p(a, method, param, maxiter=300, maxfun=5000, n=None):
    if n is None: n=len(a)
    # scale
    f=6962.0/max(n,1)
    mit=max(10,int(min(800,maxiter*f)))
    mfun=max(50,int(min(20000,maxfun*f)))
    a0=normalize_nonneg(a)
    p=p_from_a(a0)
    if method=='lse':
       fun=lbfgs_lse_p; args=(float(param),)
    elif method=='topk':
       fun=lbfgs_topk_p; args=(int(param),)
    else: return exact_score(a0),a0
    start=time.monotonic()
    try:
       res=minimize(fun,p0,jac=True,args=args,method='L-BFGS-B',options={'maxiter':mit,'maxfun':mfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-9,'maxls':30})
       a_new=softmax_from_p(res.x)
    except Exception:
       a_new=a0
    sc=exact_score(a_new)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
       return exact_score(a0), a0
    return sc,a_new
```
`lbfgs_lse_p(p,beta)` returns `objective_lse_p(p,beta)`. It can just be the function itself; scipy accepts. Top-level.

def exact_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return np.inf
   n=len(a)
   b=self_conv(a)
   mb=max(0,b.max())
   return 2*n*mb/(S*S)
```

def next_pow2(x):

```
def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=1e-12: return np.full_like(a,1.0/max(len(a),1))
    return a/S
```

def build_random_p(n, rng, max_harm=12):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n-1)
   p=np.zeros(n-1)
   for m in range(1,max_harm+1):
      amp=rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)/(m**0.7)
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      trend=rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3)
      p += amp*np.sin(2πm x + phase) + trend*x*(m? )
   p -= p.mean()
   p *= rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
   p = np.clip(p,-2,2)
   return p
```

Top-level `add_pool(pool, score, a, max_size=8)`:
```
def add_pool(pool, score, a, max_size=8):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    pool.append((float(score), np.array(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    if len(pool) > max_size:
        worst=0
        for i in range(1,len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > pool[worst][0]: worst=i
        del pool[worst]
```
`get_min_pool(pool)`:
```
def get_min_pool(pool):
   if not pool: return None
   idx=0
   for i in range(1,len(pool)):
      if pool[i][0] < pool[idx][0]: idx=i
   return pool[idx][1].copy()
```
`choose_pool_candidate(pool, best_a, idx)`:
```
if idx % 3 == 0: return best_a.copy()
if not pool: return best_a.copy()
# choose among top two: if idx%2==0 min else second? Simpler min.
return get_min_pool(pool)
```
No lambda.

`factor_candidate(a, power, n_steps, mix, deadline)`:
```
def factor_candidate(a, power, n_steps, mix, deadline):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if time.monotonic()>deadline: return a
    b=self_conv(a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0); bsum=b.sum(); if bsum<=0: return a
    target=(b+1e-15)**power
    target/=target.sum()
    M=get_fft_len_3n(a.size)
    Bt=np.fft.rfft(np.concatenate([target, np.zeros(M-target.size)]), M)
    best_sc=exact_score(a); best=a
    cur=a
    for it in range(n_steps):
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
        A=np.fft.rfft(cur,M)
        At=choose_sqrt_B(Bt,A)
        full=np.fft.irfft(At,M)
        win=extract_best_window(full, len(cur))
        s=win.sum()
        if not finite or s<1e-12:
           continue
        win=win/s
        cand=(1-mix)*cur + mix*win
        cand=normalize_nonneg(cand)
        sc=exact_score(cand)

Let's implement `build_random_p(n, rng, max_harm=10)`:
```
def build_random_p(n, rng, max_harm=10):
   if n<=1: return np.array([])
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n-1)
   p=np.zeros(n-1)
   for m in range(1, max_harm+1):
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1, 1.2))/(m**0.8)
       phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
       # random linear trend
       trend=float(rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6))
       p += amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*m*x + phase) + trend*x*(1.0/m)
   p -= p.mean()
   p *= float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.25))
   p = np.clip(p, -3.0, 3.0)
   return p
```
This yields smooth. Could occasionally include high freq? no.

    n=p.size+1

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    p=np.clip(p,-100.0,100.0)
    maxp=max(0.0,float(np.max(p)))
    e0=np.exp(-maxp)
    er=np.exp(p-maxp)
    s=e0+float(np.sum(er))
    if not finite or s<=0: return full
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=e0/s; a[1:]=er/s

    ss=a.sum()
    if ss<=0: return full
    return a/ss
```

Now, factor target `choose_sqrt_B`: vectorized sign:
```
def choose_sqrt_B(B, Aref):
    R=np.sqrt(B.astype(np.complex128))
    if R.size>1:
       d_use=np.abs(R - Aref)
       d_flip=np.abs(-R - Aref)
       R=np.where(d_flip < d_use, -R, R)
    if R.size>0:
       r0=R[0]
       R[0]=np.sqrt(max(0.0, float(np.real(B[0]))))  # positive
    if R.size>1:
       k=R.size-1
       rn=B[k]
       # Nyquist should be real
       rv=float(np.real(rn))
       if rv < 0:
           # sqrt of negative at Nyquist? For conjugate symmetry, Nyquist real can be negative. Principal sqrt gives imaginary; but Aref Nyquist real. If B[k] negative, no real root; choose imaginary? But A^2 at Nyquist can be negative if time sequence? For rfft length M, Nyquist coefficient A[Nyq] real; its square nonnegative. Thus B[k] should be nonnegative if B from A^2. Target B may have small negative due numerical? If negative, choose +sqrt(abs)? That changes square sign? Actually A Nyquist real, square ≥0. If target B negative, cannot exact. Choose real sqrt of abs to minimize? 
       rv=max(0.0, rv)
       rr=np.sqrt(rv)
       # choose sign matching Aref[k].real
       if abs(-rr - Aref[k].real) < abs(rr - Aref[k].real): rr=-rr
       R[k]=complex(rr,0.0)
    return R
```
But vectorized sign includes DC and Nyq; then we override.
For B[k] negative non-Nyq, sqrt principal gives imaginary; sign ±. That's allowed A can be complex at those frequencies. Good.
B[0] is positive real target sum? If B[0] maybe 1+ slight; sqrt positive.

```
def extract_best_window(x,n):
   M=len(x)
   if M<=n:
      y=x[:n] if n<=M else np.resize? no; return np.maximum(x,0)
   xp=np.maximum(np.asarray(x,float),0.0)
   xx=np.concatenate((xp, xp[:M-1]))
   pref=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(xx)))
   # Need sums length M: pref[n:n+M] - pref[:M]. xx length 2M-1. n+M <= 2M-1 if n <= M-1. For M>n yes.
   sums=pref[n:n+M] - pref[:M]
   s=int(np.argmax(sums))
   if s+n <= M:
      y=x[s:s+n]
   else:
      y=np.concatenate((x[s:], x[:s+n-M]))
   return np.maximum(y,0.0)
```
For M=1,n=1: M<=n, return max(x,0). Good.

def next_pow2(x):

    if n==0: return np.array([])
    M=get_len_2n(n)

`exact_score`:
```
def exact_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.inf
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   S=float(np.sum(a))
   if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
   b=self_conv(a)
   if b.size==0: return np.inf
   mb=float(np.max(np.maximum(b,0.0)))
   return float(2.0*a.size*mb/(S*S))
```

`grad_L_from_A`:
```
def grad_L_from_A(A, p, M, n):
    C=2*n-1
    p_rev=np.zeros(M,float)
    p_rev[:C]=p[::-1]
    Pr=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,M)
    r=np.fft.irfft(A*Pr, M)
    return 2.0*r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
If n=1, slice n-1=0 to 2*n-1=1. Good.

def objective_lse_p(p, beta):
    if time.monotonic() > _HARD_DEADLINE: raise HardTimeout
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    if p.size==0: return (2.0, np.array([])) # n=1 score? a=[1], conv=[1], S=1 ->2
    if not all finite: return 1e100, zeros
    p_clip=np.clip(p,-100,100)
    a=softmax_from_p(p_clip)
    n=p.size+1
    beta=float(beta)
    M=get_len_3n(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    if not all finite: ...
    bmax=float(b.max())
    if beta<=0: p_weights = ...? Use gamma? For beta<=0, uniform? But beta positive.
    shifted=beta*(b-bmax)
    shifted=np.clip(shifted,-700,0)
    e=np.exp(shifted)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       pw=np.zeros(b.size); pw[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0; L=bmax
    else:
       pw=e/s; L=bmax+np.log(s)/beta
    g_L=grad_L_from_A(A,pw,M,n)
    scale=2.0*n
    g_a=scale*g_L
    dot=float(np.dot(a,g_a))
    g_full=a*(g_a-dot)
    g_out=g_full[1:]
    # zero at clipped p
    if np.any(p_clip != p):
       g_out = np.where(p_clip != p, 0.0, g_out)
    g_out=np.nan_to_num(g_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    val=scale*L
    if not finite val: val=1e100; g_out=zeros
    return val,g_out

`run_lbfgs_p`:
```
def run_lbfgs_p(a, method, param, maxiter, maxfun, n, hard_deadline):
    global _HARD_DEADLINE, _LAST_P
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    sc0=exact_score(a)
    p=p_from_a(a)
    if p.size==0:
       return sc0,a
    # scale iterations
    factor=6962.0/max(n,1)
    mit=int(max(10, min(800, maxiter*factor)))
    mfun=int(max(100, min(20000, maxfun*factor)))
    _HARD_DEADLINE=float(hard_deadline)
    _LAST_P=None
    if method.startswith('l'):
       fun=objective_lse_p; args=(float(param),)
    elif method.startswith('t'):
       K=int(param); fun=objective_topk_p; args=(K,)
    else:
       return sc0,a
    opts={'maxiter':mit,'maxfun':mfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-9,'maxls':30}
    try:
       res=minimize(fun,p,jac=True,args=args,method='L-BFGS-B', callback=minimize_time_callback, options=opts)
       p_out=res.x
    except HardTimeout:
       p_out=_LAST_P if _LAST_P is not None else p
    except Exception:
       p_out=p
    if p_out is None: p_out=p
    a_new=softmax_from_p(p_out)
    a_new=normalize_nonneg(a_new)
    sc=exact_score(a_new)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
       return sc0,a
    return sc,a_new
```
If objective raises HardTimeout, `_LAST_P` may be None if first callback not reached; use p.
But `minimize_time_callback` itself checks deadline and raises HardTimeout after setting _LAST_P. Need define:
```
def minimize_time_callback(res):
    global _LAST_P
    try:
        _LAST_P = np.array(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        _LAST_P = None
    if time.monotonic() >= _HARD_DEADLINE:
        raise HardTimeout
```
If _HARD_DEADLINE inf, no raise.

```
def peak_coordinate_descent(a, time_limit=120, top_peaks=12, top_contrib=6, iterations=3):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); best=a; bestsc=exact
   t0=time.monotonic()
   for sweep in range(iterations):
      b=self_conv(a)
      C=len(b)
      if top_peaks >= C: pidx=np.arange(C)
      else: pidx=np.argpartition(b, C-top_peaks)[C-top_peaks:]
      pidx=pidx[np.argsort(b)::-1]
      for m in pidx:
         i_start=max(0,m-n+1); i_end=min(m,n-1)
         if i_end < i_start: continue
         # contributions = a[m]?? b_m=∑ a_i a_{m-i}. For each i in range, contrib = a[i]*a[m-i]. To reduce peak, scale the larger of pair? We can vary a_i.
         # top contributors by a[i]*a[m-i]
         contrib=np.array([a[i]*a[m-i] for i in range(i_start,i_end+1)])
         top_local=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1][:top_contrib]
         top_global=i_start+top_local
         for i in top_global:
            old=a[i]
            if old<1e-12: continue
            for factor in [0.4,0.7,0.9,1.2,1.6]:
               a[i]=old*factor
               sc=exact_score(a)
               if finite and sc<bestsc:
                   bestsc=sc; best=a.copy(); a=best; break
               a[i]=old
            if time>... break
         if time>... break
      if time>... break
   return best
```

Downsample function:
```
def downsample_seq(a, n_new):
    n_old=len(a)
    if n_old <= n_new: return a.copy()
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n_old)
    y=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    out=np.interp(y,x,a)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)
```

def minimize_time_callback(res):
    global _LAST_P
    try:
        _LAST_P = np.array(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        _LAST_P = None
    if time.monotonic() >= _HARD_DEADLINE:
        raise HardTimeout

I planned `time_limit` absolute? Let's design `peak_coordinate_descent(a, deadline, top_peaks, top_contrib, sweeps)`:
```
def peak_coordinate_descent(a, deadline, top_peaks, top_contrib, sweeps):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    best=a.copy(); bestsc=exact_score(a)
    n=a.size
    for sweep in range(sweeps):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       b=self_conv(a)
       C=b.size
       if top_peaks <=0: continue
       if top_peaks >= C: pidx=np.arange(C)
       else: pidx=np.argpartition(b, C-top_peaks)[C-top_peaks:]
       pidx=pidx[np.argsort(b[pidx])[::-1]] # maybe already unsorted; sort descending
       for m in pidx:
          if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
          m=int(m)
          ist=max(0,m-n+1); ien=min(m,n-1)
          if ien<ist: continue
          # build contrib efficiently without Python list? use slice
          # j = ist..ien; m-j from m-ist downto m-ien
          ii=np.arange(ist, ien+1)
          contrib=a[ii]*a[m-ii]
          if contrib.size <= top_contrib: order=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
          else: order=np.argpartition(contrib, -top_contrib)[-top_contrib:]
          order=order[np.argsort(contrib[order])[::-1]]
          for oi in order[:top_contrib]:
              if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
              i=int(ii[oi])
              old=a[i]
              if old < 1e-14: continue
              for factor in (0.5,0.75,0.9,1.25,1.6):
                 a[i]=old*factor
                 sc=exact_score(a)
                 if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < bestsc:
                     bestsc=sc; best=a.copy(); a=best
                     break
                 a[i]=old
    # final normalize? exact_score doesn't modify. Return best normalized.
    return normalize_nonneg(best)
```
If a normalized, after improvement a=best normalized. Good.
`a[ii]*a[m-ii]`: m-ii array within [0,n-1] by ist/ien. Good.
Use top_contrib maybe 8.

This coordinate descent modifies one coordinate; since scale invariant, changing coordinate and not renormalizing changes relative all; exact_score handles. If factor improves, keep. Good.

Now, `factor_candidate`: `n_steps` and `deadline`; no nested.
```
def factor_candidate(a, power, n_steps, mix, deadline):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a.size==0: return a
    if time.monotonic()>deadline: return a
    b=self_conv(a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    bs=b.sum()
    if bs <= 1e-300: return a
    b=b/bs
    yy=(b+1e-15)**float(power)
    s=yy.sum()
    if s<=0: return a
    target=yy/s
    M=get_len_3n(a.size)
    pad=np.zeros(M)
    pad[:target.size]=target
    Bt=np.fft.rfft(pad, M)
    best=a.copy(); bestsc=exact_score(a)
    cur=a.copy()
    for it in range(int(n_steps)):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      A=np.fft.rfft(cur, M)
      At=choose_sqrt_B(Bt, A)
      full=np.fft.irfft(At, M)
      win=extract_best_window(full, a.size)
      ws=win.sum()
      if not finite or ws<1e-12: continue
      win=win/ws
      cand=(1-mix)*cur + mix*win
      cand=normalize_nonneg(cand)
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      sc=exact_score(cand)
      if finite and sc<bestsc: bestsc=sc; best=cand.copy()
      cur=cand
    return normalize_nonng(best)
```
`mix` maybe 0.6. If candidate worse, still cur=cand, may go awry. But steps few; best kept. Could if sc > best*1.2 break? Not necessary.

`choose_sqrt_B`: Ensure `B` and `Aref` same length. B rfft of pad length M; A rfft length M. Good.
```
def choose_sqrt_B(B, Aref):
    R=np.sqrt(B.astype(np.complex128))
    if R.size > 1:
        # for k=1 to size-2 maybe include DC/Nyq but override later
        d_use=np.abs(R - Aref)
        d_flip=np.abs(-R - Aref)
        R=np.where(d_flip < d_use, -R, R)
    if R.size > 0:
        B0=float(np.real(B[0]))
        if B0 < 0: B0=0.0
        R[0]=complex(np.sqrt(B0),0.0)
    if R.size > 1:
        k=R.size-1
        rv=float(np.real(B[k]))
        if rv < 0: rv=0.0
        rr=np.sqrt(rv)
        refreal=float(np.real(Aref[k]))
        if abs(-rr-refreal) < abs(rr-refreal): rr=-rr
        R[k]=complex(rr,0.0)
    return R
```
If B[k] complex at Nyquist? rfft of real has Nyquist real; small imag ignored. Good.

Potential issue: For non-Nyq with B negative, `np.sqrt` principal gives +i*sqrt. Sign choice ±. This root squared equals B. Good.

Now, if target Bt has small negative due numerical and Aref sign, root maybe. okay.

Now, main candidate creation code in detail:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s)
    safety = 40.0 if total > 600 else max(5.0, 0.05*total)
    deadline=t0+total-safety
    if deadline < t0+10: deadline=t0+total-2
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if p is not None:
          p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          if p.size>0:
             parent=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is not None and parent.sum() > 1e-12:
       n=parent.size
       if n > 30000:
          parent = downsample_seq(parent, 6962)
          n=parent.size
       a0=normalize_nonneg(parent)
    else:
       n=6962
       a0=softmax_from_p(build_random_p(n,rng,12))
    best_a=a0; best_sc=exact_score(best_a)
    print...
    pool=[]
    add_pool(pool,best_sc,best_a,16)
    # quick start
    quick_end = t0 + min(600.0, 0.30*max(1.0,total-safety))
    starts=[]
    starts.append(best_a.copy())
    for sig in (0.02,0.06,0.15,0.4):
       if n>1:
          starts.append(log_noise_a(best_a, sig, rng))
    for _ in range(4):
       starts.append(softmax_from_p(build_random_p(n,rng,12)))
    for power in (0.9,0.75,0.6):
       if time.monotonic() < quick_end - 30:
          fa=factor_candidate(best_a, power, 10, 0.5, quick_end)
          starts.append(fa)
    for st in starts:
       if time.monotonic() > quick_end: break
       sc,a1=run_lbfgs_p(st,'lse',1000,120,1200,n,quick_end)
       add_pool(pool,sc,a1,16)
       if sc < best_sc: update
       sc,a2=run_lbfgs_p(st,'topk',64,120,1200,n,quick_end)
       add_pool...
    # main
    configs=[...]
    cycle=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       cfg=configs[cycle%(len(configs))]
       method=cfg[0]; param=cfg[1]; mit=cfg[2]; mfun=cfg[3]
       # factor escape
       if cycle % 14 == 7 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 250:
           power = 0.85 - 0.05*((cycle//14)%3)
           fa=factor_candidate(best_a, power, 15, 0.5, deadline-20)
           sc=exact_score(fa); add_pool; update
           sc,a1=run_lbfgs_p(fa,'lse',5000,300,3000,n,deadline-10)
           add/update
       else:
           if cycle % 3 == 0:
              a_start=best_a.copy()
           else:
              a_start=get_min_pool(pool)
              if a_start is None: a_start=best_a.copy()
           sc,a_new=run_lbfgs_p(a_start, method, param, mit, mfun, n, deadline-5)
           add_pool; update
       cycle += 1
    # final CD
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80:
       a_cd=peak_coordinate_descent(best_a, deadline-30, 16, 8, 3)
       sc=exact; update
    # final polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
       sc,a1=run_lbfgs_p(best_a,'lse',20000,400,4000,n,deadline-3)
       update
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
       sc,a2=run_lbfgs_p(best_a,'topk',1,250,2500,n,deadline-3)
       update
    out loop
    verify
    return out
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    F(a) = 2 n max_k (a * a)[k] / (sum a)^2.

The implementation uses:
  - a gauge-fixed log-simplex parameterization (z[0] fixed to 0) to optimize
    scale-invariant objectives without non-negativity projection;
  - LSE-smoothed and top-k objective variants for L-BFGS;
  - a spectral square-root / target-convolution projection for basin escape;
  - peak coordinate descent for final micro-polish.

The parent construction, if present, is used as the primary warm start,
but multiple smooth random/noisy restarts are also explored.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


class HardTimeout(Exception):
    pass


_LEN_CACHE = {}
_HARD_DEADLINE = float("inf")
_LAST_P = None


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def get_len_2n(n):
    key = (2, int(n))
    val = _LEN_CACHE.get(key)
    if val is None:
        val = next_pow2(2 * int(n) - 1)
        _LEN_CACHE[key] = val
    return val


def get_len_3n(n):
    key = (3, int(n))
    val = _LEN_CACHE.get(key)
    if val is None:
        val = next_pow2(3 * int(n) - 2)
        _LEN_CACHE[key] = val
    return val


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def downsample_seq(a, n_new):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_old = a.size
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_new <= 1:
        return np.array([float(a.flat[0]) if n_old else 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n_old <= 1:
        return np.full(n_new, float(a.flat[0]) if n_old else 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n_old <= n_new:
        return a.copy()
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(y, x, a).astype(np.float64)


def self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = get_len_2n(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:2 * n - 1]
    return c


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    b = self_conv(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mb = float(np.max(np.maximum(b, 0.0)))
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mb / (s * s))


def softmax_from_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size + 1
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.clip(p, -100.0, 100.0)
    maxp = max(0.0, float(np.max(p)))
    e0 = np.exp(-maxp)
    er = np.exp(p - maxp)
    s = e0 + float(er.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out[0] = e0 / s
    out[1:] = er / s
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / ss


def p_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    p = np.log(a[1:] / a[0])
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    return np.clip(p, -100.0, 100.0).astype(np.float64)


def grad_L_from_A(A, p, M, n):
    C = 2 * n - 1
    p_rev = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    p_rev[:C] = p[::-1]
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, M)
    r = np.fft.irfft(A * Pr, M)
    return 2.0 * r[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def objective_lse_p(p, beta):
    if time.monotonic() > _HARD_DEADLINE:
        raise HardTimeout

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size + 1
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        beta = 1e-12

    p_clip = np.clip(p, -100.0, 100.0)
    a = softmax_from_p(p_clip)
    M = get_len_3n(n)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)

    bmax = float(b.max())
    shifted = beta * (b - bmax)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        pw = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        pw[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        L = bmax
    else:
        pw = e / s
        L = bmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta

    gL = grad_L_from_A(A, pw, M, n)
    scale = 2.0 * n
    ga = scale * gL
    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gf = a * (ga - dot)
    gout = gf[1:].copy()

    bad = p_clip != p
    if np.any(bad):
        gout[bad] = 0.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gout)):
        gout = np.where(np.isfinite(gout), gout, 0.0)
    gout = np.clip(gout, -1e6, 1e6)

    val = float(scale * L)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return val, gout


def objective_topk_p(p, K):
    if time.monotonic() > _HARD_DEADLINE:
        raise HardTimeout

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size + 1
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)

    K = int(K)
    p_clip = np.clip(p, -100.0, 100.0)
    a = softmax_from_p(p_clip)
    M = get_len_3n(n)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)

    C = b.size
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= C:
        idx = np.arange(C)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, C - K)[C - K:]

    pw = np.zeros(C, dtype=np.float64)
    pw[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
    score = float(np.mean(b[idx]))

    gL = grad_L_from_A(A, pw, M, n)
    scale = 2.0 * n
    ga = scale * gL
    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gf = a * (ga - dot)
    gout = gf[1:].copy()

    bad = p_clip != p
    if np.any(bad):
        gout[bad] = 0.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gout)):
        gout = np.where(np.isfinite(gout), gout, 0.0)
    gout = np.clip(gout, -1e6, 1e6)

    val = float(scale * score)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return val, gout


def minimize_time_callback(res):
    global _LAST_P
    try:
        _LAST_P = np.array(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        _LAST_P = None
    if time.monotonic() >= _HARD_DEADLINE:
        raise HardTimeout


def run_lbfgs_p(a, method, param, maxiter, maxfun, n, hard_deadline):
    global _HARD_DEADLINE, _LAST_P

    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    sc0 = exact_score(a)
    p = p_from_a(a)
    if p.size == 0:
        return sc0, a

    factor = 6962.0 / max(int(n), 1)
    mit = int(max(10, min(800, maxiter * factor)))
    mfun = int(max(100, min(20000, maxfun * factor)))

    _HARD_DEADLINE = float(hard_deadline)
    _LAST_P = None

    if str(method).startswith("l"):
        fun = objective_lse_p
        args = (float(param),)
    elif str(method).startswith("t"):
        fun = objective_topk_p
        args = (int(param),)
    else:
        return sc0, a

    opts = {
        "maxiter": mit,
        "maxfun": mfun,
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "maxls": 30,
    }

    p_out = p
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun,
            p,
            jac=True,
            args=args,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=minimize_time_callback,
            options=opts,
        )
        p_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except HardTimeout:
        if _LAST_P is not None:
            p_out = np.asarray(_LAST_P, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p_out = p
    except Exception:
        p_out = p

    if p_out is None or p_out.size != p.size:
        p_out = p

    a_new = softmax_from_p(p_out)
    a_new = normalize_nonneg(a_new)
    sc = exact_score(a_new)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return sc0, a
    return sc, a_new


def build_random_p(n, rng, max_harm=12):
    n = int(n)
    m = max(0, n - 1)
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for h in range(1, int(max_harm) + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 1.25)) / (float(h) ** 0.8)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        trend = float(rng.uniform(-0.55, 0.55)) / float(h)
        p += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(h) * x + phase) + trend * x
    p -= float(np.mean(p))
    p *= float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.25))
    return np.clip(p, -3.5, 3.5).astype(np.float64)


def log_noise_a(a, sigma, rng):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 1:
        return np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = p_from_a(a)
    if p.size:
        p = p + float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(p.size)
    return softmax_from_p(p)


def choose_sqrt_B(B, Aref):
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.complex128)
    Aref = np.asarray(Aref, dtype=np.complex128)
    R = np.sqrt(B)
    if R.size > 1:
        d_use = np.abs(R - Aref)
        d_flip = np.abs(-R - Aref)
        R = np.where(d_flip < d_use, -R, R)
    if R.size > 0:
        r0 = float(np.real(B[0]))
        if r0 < 0.0:
            r0 = 0.0
        R[0] = complex(np.sqrt(r0), 0.0)
    if R.size > 1:
        k = R.size - 1
        rn = float(np.real(B[k]))
        if rn < 0.0:
            rn = 0.0
        rr = np.sqrt(rn)
        ref = float(np.real(Aref[k]))
        if abs(-rr - ref) < abs(rr - ref):
            rr = -rr
        R[k] = complex(rr, 0.0)
    return R


def extract_best_window(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    M = int(x.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if M <= n:
        return np.maximum(x, 0.0).copy()
    xp = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    xx = np.concatenate((xp, xp[:M - 1]))
    pref = np.empty(xx.size + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    pref[1:] = np.cumsum(xx)
    sums = pref[n:n + M] - pref[:M]
    s = int(np.argmax(sums))
    if s + n <= M:
        y = x[s:s + n]
    else:
        y = np.concatenate((x[s:], x[:s + n - M]))
    return np.maximum(y, 0.0)


def factor_candidate(a, power, n_steps, mix, deadline):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1 or time.monotonic() > deadline:
        return a

    b = self_conv(a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    bs = float(b.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(bs) or bs <= 1e-300:
        return a
    b = b / bs

    yy = (b + 1e-15) ** float(power)
    ys = float(yy.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ys) or ys <= 1e-300:
        return a
    target = yy / ys

    M = get_len_3n(n)
    pad = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    pad[:target.size] = target
    Bt = np.fft.rfft(pad, M)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    cur = a.copy()
    mix = float(mix)
    mix = max(0.01, min(0.99, mix))

    for _ in range(int(n_steps)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        A = np.fft.rfft(cur, M)
        At = choose_sqrt_B(Bt, A)
        full = np.fft.irfft(At, M)
        win = extract_best_window(full, n)
        ws = float(win.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-12:
            continue
        win = win / ws
        cand = (1.0 - mix) * cur + mix * win
        cand = normalize_nonneg(cand)
        sc = exact_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = cand.copy()
        cur = cand

    return normalize_nonneg(best)


def add_pool(pool, score, a, max_size=16):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    pool.append((float(score), a.copy()))
    if len(pool) > int(max_size):
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > pool[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        del pool[worst]


def get_min_pool(pool):
    if not pool:
        return None
    idx = 0
    for i in range(1, len(pool)):
        if pool[i][0] < pool[idx][0]:
            idx = i
    return pool[idx][1].copy()


def peak_coordinate_descent(a, deadline, top_peaks, top_contrib, sweeps):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    top_peaks = max(1, int(top_peaks))
    top_contrib = max(1, int(top_contrib))
    sweeps = max(0, int(sweeps))

    for _ in range(sweeps):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        b = self_conv(a)
        C = int(b.size)
        if C == 0:
            break
        if top_peaks >= C:
            pidx = np.arange(C)
        else:
            pidx = np.argpartition(b, C - top_peaks)[C - top_peaks:]
        pidx = pidx[np.argsort(b[pidx])[::-1]]

        for m in pidx:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            m = int(m)
            ist = max(0, m - n + 1)
            ien = min(m, n - 1)
            if ien < ist:
                continue
            ii = np.arange(ist, ien + 1)
            contrib = a[ii] * a[m - ii]
            if contrib.size <= top_contrib:
                order = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1]
            else:
                order = np.argpartition(contrib, -top_contrib)[-top_contrib:]
                order = order[np.argsort(contrib[order])[::-1]]

            for oi in order[:top_contrib]:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                i = int(ii[oi])
                old = a[i]
                if old <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                for f in (0.5, 0.72, 0.88, 1.25, 1.65):
                    a[i] = old * float(f)
                    sc = exact_score(a)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best = a.copy()
                        break
                    a[i] = old

    return normalize_nonneg(best)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    safety = 40.0 if total > 600 else max(5.0, 0.05 * total)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, total - safety)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0:
                parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and float(np.sum(parent)) > 1e-12:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n > 30000:
            parent = downsample_seq(parent, 6962)
            n = int(parent.size)
        a0 = normalize_nonneg(parent)
    else:
        n = 6962
        a0 = softmax_from_p(build_random_p(n, rng, 12))

    best_a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best_sc = exact_score(best_a)
    print(f"initial n={n}, score={best_sc:.10f}")

    pool = []
    add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)

    quick_end = t0 + min(650.0, 0.30 * max(1.0, total - safety))

    starts = [best_a.copy()]
    for sig in (0.02, 0.06, 0.15, 0.35):
        starts.append(log_noise_a(best_a, sig, rng))
    for _ in range(4):
        starts.append(softmax_from_p(build_random_p(n, rng, 12)))
    for pw in (0.90, 0.75, 0.60):
        if time.monotonic() < quick_end - 30:
            starts.append(factor_candidate(best_a, pw, 10, 0.5, quick_end))

    for st in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > quick_end:
            break

        sc, a1 = run_lbfgs_p(st, "lse", 1000, 120, 1200, n, quick_end)
        add_pool(pool, sc, a1, 16)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_nonneg(a1)
            add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
            print(f"quick lse improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

        sc, a2 = run_lbfgs_p(st, "topk", 64, 120, 1200, n, quick_end)
        add_pool(pool, sc, a2, 16)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_nonneg(a2)
            add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
            print(f"quick topk improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

    print(f"after quick stage: {best_sc:.10f}")

    configs = [
        ("lse", 500, 220, 2200),
        ("topk", 128, 220, 2200),
        ("lse", 2000, 260, 2600),
        ("topk", 32, 260, 2600),
        ("lse", 8000, 260, 2600),
        ("topk", 8, 260, 2600),
        ("lse", 30000, 300, 3000),
        ("topk", 2, 300, 3000),
        ("lse", 100000, 320, 3200),
        ("topk", 1, 320, 3200),
    ]

    cycle = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        method, param, mit, mfun = configs[cycle % len(configs)]

        if cycle % 18 == 9 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 260:
            pw = 0.85 - 0.05 * ((cycle // 18) % 3)
            fa = factor_candidate(best_a, pw, 15, 0.5, deadline - 25)
            sc = exact_score(fa)
            add_pool(pool, sc, fa, 16)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_nonneg(fa)
                add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
                print(f"factor improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

            sc, a1 = run_lbfgs_p(fa, "lse", 4000, 300, 3000, n, deadline - 12)
            add_pool(pool, sc, a1, 16)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_nonneg(a1)
                add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
                print(f"factor+lse improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")
        else:
            if cycle % 3 == 0:
                a_start = best_a.copy()
            else:
                a_start = get_min_pool(pool)
                if a_start is None:
                    a_start = best_a.copy()

            sc, a_new = run_lbfgs_p(a_start, method, param, mit, mfun, n, deadline - 5)
            add_pool(pool, sc, a_new, 16)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = normalize_nonneg(a_new)
                add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
                print(f"main {method}/{param} improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

        cycle += 1

    print(f"before final local polish: {best_sc:.10f}")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80:
        acd = peak_coordinate_descent(best_a, deadline - 30, 16, 8, 3)
        sc = exact_score(acd)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_nonneg(acd)
            add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
            print(f"coordinate descent improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
        sc, a1 = run_lbfgs_p(best_a, "lse", 20000, 400, 4000, n, deadline - 3)
        add_pool(pool, sc, a1, 16)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_nonneg(a1)
            add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
            print(f"final lse improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
        sc, a2 = run_lbfgs_p(best_a, "topk", 1, 250, 2500, n, deadline - 3)
        add_pool(pool, sc, a2, 16)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = normalize_nonneg(a2)
            add_pool(pool, best_sc, best_a, 16)
            print(f"final topk improvement: {best_sc:.10f} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")

    out = []
    for x in best_a:
        v = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            v = 0.0
        if v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        elif v > 1000.0:
            v = 1000.0
        out.append(v)

    if len(out) == 0 or float(np.sum(out)) < 1e-6:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print("=" * 64)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, n={len(out)}, "
          f"elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 64)
    return out
```
